| amex-011 | 2-legged OAuth uses a JWT client assertion (RFC 7523 style) signed with the client secret using an HMAC-SHA algorithm, sent with grant_type=client_credentials and the X-AMEX-API-KEY header carrying the client_id. | developer | OAuth 2.0 Client Credentials Flow (2Legged) \| American Express Developers | American Express | https://developer.americanexpress.com/documentation/api-security/oauth-client-credentials-flow | 2026-06-01 | 2026-09-25 | Verified |  | Quote: "The Client Assertion is a JWT signed with the Client Secret (provided via the American Express Developers Portal) using any recommended HMAC SHA algorithm" · Required claims iss, sub, aud ([https://americanexpress.com]), exp, jti; client_assertion_type urn:ietf:params:oauth:client-assertion-type:jwt-bearer; headers X-CorrelationID (UUID), X-AMEX-API-KEY ('This is a client_id value and will be provided via the American Express Developers Portal.'), Content-Type application/x-www-form-urlencoded. The spec viewer supports four security models: oneWayTLS_AppKey, oAuth, oneWayTLS_HMAC, mTLS_HMAC (bundle). · fetch curl · verified 2026-09-25 |

| amex-258 | Amex first tries to resolve a disputed transaction from its own information, then sends either a Chargeback or an Inquiry; an unanswered Inquiry becomes a 'No Reply' Chargeback, and reason codes are grouped into Authorisation, Cardmember Disputes, Fraud, Inquiry/Miscellaneous, Processing Error and Fraud Full Recourse. | rules | Merchant Regulations – International, April 2026, Sections 5.4–5.8 | American Express | https://www.americanexpress.com/content/dam/amex/us/merchant/new-merchant-regulations/Regs_EN.pdf | 2026-04-17 | 2026-09-25 | Verified |  | Quote: "If you choose not to respond to our Inquiry, we will debit your Merchant Account with a "No Reply" Chargeback" · Codes are dual-labelled ISO plus alpha code, e.g. Card Not Present fraud 'ISO 4540/F29', goods not received 'ISO 4554/C08', Fraud Full Recourse 'ISO 4763 / FR2'. | amex-261 | Merchants enter the Fraud Full Recourse (FFR) Program, losing all fraud-chargeback reversal rights and SafeKey liability shift, when monthly fraud-to-gross reaches 0.9% with USD 25,000 fraud (Low Tier) or 1.8% with USD 50,000 (High Tier). | rules | Merchant Regulations – U.S., October 2024, Table 10-42 FTG Performance Tiers | American Express (PDF mirrored on RealPage legal page) | https://www.realpage.com/storage/files/pages/legal/pdfs/2025/05/amex-merchant-regulations.pdf | 2024-10 | 2026-09-25 | Verified |  | Quote: "The monthly fraud to gross* Charges ratio at an Establishment equals or exceeds 0.9% and" · Low Tier: three consecutive months to remediate after notice; High Tier: FFR applies from notice. ## Contradictions

- How It Works page: 'For some of our public products, the Sandbox APIs are automatically accessible without reaching out to us.' · versus · Product catalogue endpoint returns instantSandboxAccess:false for all 18 published products; dashboard says keys are granted after team review. · https://developer.americanexpress.com/documentation/how-it-works · https://a4dextsvr.americanexpress.com/dev-portal/a4d/v1/products/
- ACE overview access form: 'Please submit this form to request access to the API Guide and Specifications.' · versus · The ACE OpenAPI 3.1.0 specification (286 KB) and guide JSON are downloadable logged out from the public content host. · https://developer.americanexpress.com/products/nextgen-agentic-payments/overview · https://icm.aexp-static.com/Internet/EnterpriseAPIPortal/products/nextgen-agentic-payments/nextgen-agentic-payments-openapi-specification.json
- targeted-offers-client README installs '@americanexpress/targeted-offers-client' from npm. · versus · registry.npmjs.org returns {"error":"Not found"} for that package and the @americanexpress scope has 0 packages. · https://github.com/americanexpress/targeted-offers-client · https://registry.npmjs.org/@americanexpress%2Ftargeted-offers-client
- amex-api-java-client-core README (2016, unchanged): 'Artifacts are coming soon to JCenter. Stay tuned!' · versus · No artifacts on Maven Central (0 results for io.aexp) and issue #5 'Offer artifacts in Maven Central' open since 2018-12-06. · https://github.com/americanexpress/amex-api-java-client-core · https://search.maven.org/solrsearch/select?q=g:io.aexp*&rows=20&wt=json
- GNS Developers page: 'Our products are backed by a team of passionate American Express developers who are adding new code regularly.' · versus · Official API client repos last received code commits in 2018 (.NET), 2020 (Java) and 2022 (Node); NuGet package last published 2018-06-13. · https://network.americanexpress.com/globalnetwork/v4/partners/developers/ · https://github.com/americanexpress/amex-api-dotnet-client-core
- GNS Service Providers page (2026): 'join over 1200 Service Providers' · versus · AMEX Enabled Overview PDF (June 2022): '800+ SERVICE PROVIDERS' (dated difference, both Amex-published). · https://network.americanexpress.com/globalnetwork/v4/partners/service-providers/ · https://network.americanexpress.com/globalnetwork/dam/jcr:3c36b10b-65e2-4028-93a6-106428c2a7f6/34435%20-%20GNS%20-%20Amex%20Enabled%20-%20Overview_7.1.2022-11-03-01-25-20.pdf
- GNS Developers page lists four product categories (Fraud prevention, Open banking, Payment services, Personalized services). · versus · Catalogue endpoint returns five categories including 'Utilities' (Amex Account Connect, Network Loyalty, OAuth Security). · https://network.americanexpress.com/globalnetwork/v4/partners/developers/ · https://a4dextsvr.americanexpress.com/dev-portal/a4d/v1/products/
- GNS service-providers page (2026): 'join over 1200 Service Providers who have developed, certified and deployed their products' · versus · AMEX Enabled Service Provider Program overview PDF (June 2022, still linked): '800+ SERVICE PROVIDERS' · https://network.americanexpress.com/globalnetwork/v4/partners/service-providers/ · https://network.americanexpress.com/globalnetwork/dam/jcr:3c36b10b-65e2-4028-93a6-106428c2a7f6/34435%20-%20GNS%20-%20Amex%20Enabled%20-%20Overview_7.1.pdf
- OptBlue merchant page and Sales Agent page: merchants under $3MM ECV eligible; 'Effective April 17, 2026, the merchant acquiring threshold in the U.S. has been increased to $3,000,000' · versus · Payment Facilitator factsheet (October 2024, still linked from the OptBlue page): 'the $1,000,000 ECV requirement still applies' and PayFacs must 'not sponsor individual merchants with greater than $1MM' · https://www.americanexpress.com/en-us/business/optblue/agent/ · https://www.americanexpress.com/content/dam/amex/us/merchant/pdf/Payment-Facilitator-FactSheet.pdf
- Lithic release (2025-05-14) and PYMNTS/Marqeta (2025-02-26) name the programme 'Agile Partnership Platform' · versus · American Banker (2024-01-16) and search summaries name it 'Agile Partner Platform (APP)' · https://www.lithic.com/blog/amex-announcement · https://www.americanbanker.com/news/we-are-picky-inside-amexs-fintech-strategy
- Global Payments Express Boarding help: the OptBlue ISO Registration number 'is assigned by Global Payments' · versus · Search-index snippet of the same help system: the number 'is assigned by TSYS' (pre-merger wording) · https://www.mreports.com/spmod/webhelp/111230.htm · https://www.mreports.com/spmod/webhelp/111241.htm
- Q4 2025 results newsroom boilerplate (2026-01-30): 'With over a hundred million merchant locations across our global network' · versus · 2026 Chairman's Letter: 'over 170 million merchant locations worldwide ... as of year-end 2025' (incl. ~37M China wallet merchants); Sept 2025 release: 160 million as of June 2025 · https://www.americanexpress.com/en-us/newsroom/articles/financial-news/american-express-reports-full-year-and-fourth-quarter-2025-finan.html · https://www.americanexpress.com/content/dam/amex/en-us/newsroom/pdfs/AXP-2026-Chairmans-Letter-to-Shareholders.pdf
- Amex newsroom, 2026-04-16 Hyper release: 'leveraging the Agile Partner Platform' · versus · Lithic (quoting Amex SVP Will Stredwick), 2025-05-14: 'welcome Lithic to our Agile Partnership Platform' · https://www.americanexpress.com/en-us/newsroom/articles/amex-for-business/american-express-to-acquire-hyper--adding-to-its-ai-expertise-an.html · https://www.lithic.com/blog/amex-announcement
- 2025 OptBlue Program Updates PDF: OptBlue for merchants 'estimated to process under $1MM in annual American Express Charge Volume' · versus · OptBlue Sales Agent page: threshold 'increased to $3,000,000' effective April 17, 2026 · https://www.americanexpress.com/en-us/business/optblue/agent/agent-resources/2025-OptBlue-Program-Updates.pdf?v=0 · https://www.americanexpress.com/en-us/business/optblue/agent/
- Amex Wizz Air release dateline: 'London, UK, 26 June' · versus · Newsroom index firstPublishDate for the same release: 2025-07-03 · https://www.americanexpress.com/en-us/newsroom/articles/emea/american-express-acceptance-takes-off-with-wizz-air.html · https://www.americanexpress.com/en-us/newsroom/articles/emea/american-express-acceptance-takes-off-with-wizz-air.html
- Search snippet for Amex Merchant Financing: 'Fixed fees range from 1.75-7% (6-Month Term), 3.5-14% (12-Month Term), and 7-28% (24-Month Term).' · versus · Second search snippet for the same page: 'Fixed fees range from 1.75-8% (6-Month Term), 3.5-12% (12-Month Term), and 7-20% (24-Month Term).' Page could not be fetched to resolve. · https://merchantfinancing.americanexpress.com/merchantfinancing/index.htm · https://merchantfinancing.americanexpress.com/merchantfinancing/index.htm?extlink=GCP_MerFi2017_PS_G_BR_MerchantFinancing_Exact
- 2025 OptBlue Program Updates PDF: OptBlue is for 'Small Businesses who are estimated to process under $1MM in annual American Express Charge Volume' · versus · OptBlue Program Update Rev. April 2026: 'Effective April 17, 2026, the merchant acquiring threshold in the U.S. has been increased to $3,000,000.' (a dated change rather than a conflict; both documents remain live on the agent site) · https://www.americanexpress.com/en-us/business/optblue/agent/agent-resources/2025-OptBlue-Program-Updates.pdf?v=0 · https://www.americanexpress.com/en-us/business/optblue/agent/agent-resources/WRQ-945_OptBlue_SA_ProgramUpdates_2026_April.pdf?v=0

## Claims this research could not verify

- amex-020 · Product API references are OpenAPI 3.x JSON files hosted on Amex's static content host with a per-product change-log.json that the portal renders as a version-history page; the ACE change log records a single 'Launched' entry dated April 6, 2026. · page-changed · Cited /resources page now 'Page Not Found' and the portal bundle has no such route. The OpenAPI 3.1.0 JSON is still hosted on icm.aexp-static.com (x-lastUpdated April 28, 2026, version 1.0.0) and the bundle still fetches a per-product change-log.json from the content host, but every change-log.json path tried returns 404, so the quoted 'April 6, 2026 / 1.0.0' entry could not be found. · https://developer.americanexpress.com/products/nextgen-agentic-payments/resources
- amex-445 · A search-indexed copy of the GNS registration page on the QA host qnetwork.americanexpress.com describes Knowledge Base as the replacement for the earlier GNSweb extranet; the production /globalnetwork/register/ page redirects to the enterprise login and the QA host returned HTTP 503, so this could not be read on a live page. · could not be re-read by any method on 2026-09-25 · Live URL now 302s to https://identity-1-qa.americanexpress.com/en-us/account/enterprise/login (not 503); Wayback has no capture of /globalnetwork/register/ (only 2017-2020 captures of /globalnetwork/ index). The GNSweb quote could not be checked against any readable page. · https://qnetwork.americanexpress.com/globalnetwork/register/
